int __cdecl _isa_available_init()
{

  int v6; // r9d
  int v7; // esi
  int v9; // r10d
  int v14; // edi
  int v15; // eax
  int v16; // eax
  __int64 v17; // rcx
  int v18; // r8d
  int v23; // eax
  char v25; // [rsp+20h] [rbp+8h]

  _RAX = 0;
  __asm { cpuid }
  v5 = 0;
  v6 = _RBX;
  v7 = _RAX;
  _RAX = 1;
  v9 = _RCX ^ 0x6C65746E | _RDX ^ 0x49656E69;
  __asm { cpuid }
  v14 = _RCX;
  if ( (v6 ^ 0x756E6547 | v9) == 0
    && ((_memset_nt_threshold = -1, v15 = _RAX & 0xFFF3FF0, _memset_fast_string_threshold = 0x8000, v15 == 67264)
     || v15 == 132704
     || v15 == 132720
     || (unsigned int)(v16 = v15 - 198224) <= 0x20 && (v17 = 0x100010001LL, _bittest64(&v17, (unsigned __int8)v16))) )
  {
    v18 = _favor | 1;
    _favor |= 1u;
  }
  else
  {
    v18 = _favor;
  }
  _RAX = 7;
  if ( v7 >= 7 )
  {
    __asm { cpuid }
    v5 = _RBX;
    if ( (_RBX & 0x200) != 0 )
      _favor = v18 | 2;
  }
  _isa_available = 1;
  _isa_enabled = 2;
  if ( (v14 & 0x100000) != 0 )
  {
    _isa_available = 2;
    _isa_enabled = 6;
    if ( (v14 & 0x8000000) != 0 && (v14 & 0x10000000) != 0 )
    {
      __asm { xgetbv }
      v25 = _RAX;
      if ( (_RAX & 6) == 6 )
      {
        v23 = _isa_enabled | 8;
        _isa_available = 3;
        _isa_enabled |= 8u;

          _isa_enabled = v23 | 0x20;
          if ( (v5 & 0xD0030000) == 0xD0030000 && (v25 & 0xE0) == 0xE0 )
          {

            _isa_available = 6;
          }
        }
      }

// FUNC 180045590 __alloca_probe
unsigned __int64 __fastcall _alloca_probe()
{

  char *v1; // r10
  char *StackLimit; // r11
  char v3; // [rsp+18h] [rbp+8h] BYREF

  v1 = &v3 - result;
  if ( (unsigned __int64)&v3 < result )
    v1 = nullptr;
  StackLimit = (char *)NtCurrentTeb()->NtTib.StackLimit;
  if ( v1 < StackLimit )
  {
    LOWORD(v1) = (unsigned __int16)v1 & 0xF000;
    do
    {
      StackLimit -= 4096;
      *StackLimit = 0;
    }
    while ( v1 != StackLimit );
  }
  return result;
}
